Can you figure out how tuo run it from here? Are there codex specific instructons i need? Install or update desloppify, then scan this codebase for code quality issues (dead code,
duplication, complexity, smells). It tracks findings and reports a health score.

pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git
desloppify scan --path .
desloppify status
desloppify next

--path is the directory to scan (use "." for the whole project, or "src/" etc).
Language is auto-detected. To override: desloppify --lang python scan --path .
(note: --lang goes BEFORE the subcommand)

Fix what it finds, then "desloppify resolve fixed <id>" and rescan. For false positives:
"desloppify resolve wontfix <id> --note 'reason'" (note is required — don't skip work).
Keep going until all issues are resolved. If you notice anything wrong or confusing about
desloppify itself, tell me and ask if I want you to log an issue. If you need to understand
how desloppify works or debug an issue: git clone https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git /tmp/desloppify

If you're Claude Code, also run:
mkdir -p .claude/skills/desloppify && curl -sL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peteromallet/desloppify/main/SKILL.md -o .claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md

  - Auto language detection now respects `--path` when it points at a project root.

> AGENT

Yes. I logged a concrete anti-gaming issue with reproduction and implementation points:

- [Issue #102: Anti-gaming guardrails: subjective assessment import can swing objective_strict without findings](https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify/issues/102)

Key meta finding from the repro:
- `objective_strict` can move a lot from assessment-only imports (no findings/evidence required).
- `strict_score` can still read `100`, which can hide that risk in dashboards.

Where to enforce (best order):
1. `review --import --holistic` (primary gate; reject/flag assessment-only large deltas by default).
2. `status`/scorecard (clarify progress-vs-quality and warn on suspicious assessment-only shifts).
3. Optional scan-time detector for assessment drift patterns.

What we can do immediately in our own process before tooling changes:
1. Blind subjective reviewers to score output while reviewing.
2. Require evidence per dimension (related files + concrete notes) for any score increase.
3. Use 2 independent reviews and median scores.
4. Require human sign-off for large subjective jumps without corresponding findings.

If you want, I can also open a follow-up issue specifically for a “blind review mode” (hide current score from agents during review).

> AGENT

Yes, it’s confusing right now.

**Better names**
1. `score` → `Resolution Progress`  
What it is: weighted percent of findings that are not `open` (includes `wontfix`/`false_positive`).
2. `strict_score` → `Accepted Resolution Progress`  
What it is: weighted percent that are `fixed|auto_resolved|false_positive` (excludes `wontfix`).
3. `objective_score` → `Quality Health`  
What it is: detector + subjective quality blend (open findings penalize).
4. `objective_strict` → `Quality Health (Strict)`  
What it is: same as above, but `wontfix` also penalizes.

Right now, status headline uses `objective_*` (quality), while JSON still shows `strict_score: 100`, which makes this feel contradictory.

**What is blocking “100 strict” in practice**
- Mechanical dimensions are already 100.
- The gap is subjective quality:
  - AI Generated Debt 80
  - Abstraction Fit 82
  - Logic Clarity 83
  - Contracts 84
  - Naming Quality 84
  - Type Safety 85
  - Error Consistency 86
- Two open re-review flags also remain:
  - `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/ImageGenerationForm.tsx`
  - `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/hooks/useImageGenForm.ts`

**How to push each score up (concrete)**
1. AI Generated Debt (80): remove migration/legacy clutter + comment noise.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/segmentSettingsMigration.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/travelBetweenImages/types.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/useStructureVideo.ts`, plus high comment-density files like `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/index.tsx`.
2. Abstraction Fit (82): split god modules by responsibility.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/errorHandler.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/types/shots.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/utils.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/queryKeys.ts`.
3. Logic Clarity (83): break down very large orchestrator/components.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/VideoLightbox.tsx`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/TimelineContainer/TimelineContainer.tsx`.
4. Contracts (84): normalize API contracts in mixed sync/async files.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTaskBridge.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/generationTransformers.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/taskCreation.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideoService.ts`.
5. Naming Quality (84): replace vague `utils`/generic names with domain-specific ones.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/VariantSelector/utils.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/utils.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/utils.ts`.
6. Type Safety (85): remove `any` hotspots, especially in edge functions.  
Targets: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/update-task-status/index.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/complete_task/generation-child.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/calculate-task-cost/index.ts`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/complete_task/params.ts`.
7. Error Consistency (86): standardize per-layer strategy (`throw` vs `Result` vs `null`).  
Targets: shared hooks/lib where mixed patterns are common, especially around `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/hooks` and `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib`.

**Math reality**
- Subjective dimensions are 25% of quality score.
- Current subjective average is ~83.4, which explains ~95.9 objective strict.
- To hit true 100 quality strict, subjective dimensions must be near 100 across the board.
- Practical near-term target: raise subjective average to ~90 → quality strict ~97.5.

1. `errorHandler.ts` + `types/shots.ts` split,
2. strict typing pass in `supabase/functions/complete_task/*`,
3. cleanup of deprecated/migration hotspots.
